<meta>This page continues the discussion of the twelve astrological houses and their planetary associations, moving into the division of the zodiacal circle into quadrants and the elemental qualities of the signs.</meta>

they must return: for since [the ninth house] concerns a long life, it is only to be attained through law, wisdom, justice, fortitude, temperance, and the contemplation of heavenly things. Just as Jupiter <note>original: ♃</note> is the ruler of all these virtues, so he has obtained leadership of the ninth house, from which he rightly takes his name. ¶ The tenth house is dedicated to kingdoms, returning to Mars <note>original: ♂</note>, to whom belong the powers of the military, authority, severity, and wars involving death. ¶ The eleventh is the house of Fortune, returning to the highest fortune of the world. ¶ The twelfth is the house of enemies; because it is removed from the East, the East confirms it, yet it is stained because Venus <note>original: ♀</note>—the fortune of the soul—is accustomed to follow the passions. Mercury <note>original: ☿</note> returns to the East because, just as the East represents birth, life, and fear, so Mercury and the rational soul are characterized by various wanderings from the start. Mercury rejoices in the East, seeking nothing of himself outside of himself. The Moon <note>original: ☽</note> is in the third house, for the third path belongs to the Moon and travelers. Venus is in the fifth, as both Venus and that house rejoice in delights. Mars is in the sixth, as both prefer passions. The Sun <note>original: ☉</note> is in the ninth, as it represents virtue, which is truth and the contemplation of divinity. Jupiter is in the eleventh, as both carry fortune. Saturn <note>original: ♄</note> is in the twelfth, where there are extreme labors and punishments.

<image-desc>Decorative initial Q containing a circular medallion with the profile of a bearded man wearing a head covering.</image-desc>
**The Fourfold** is another designation of the quadrants of the circle according to a certain divine order. The Eastern quadrant, as it comes forth from darkness into light, is animate and incorporeal. The second, whose paths involve death, is inanimate and incorporeal. The third, as it departs from light into darkness, is corporeal and inanimate. The fourth, opposite the second, is animate and corporeal.

<margin>Also</margin> There are other mixtures of the natures of the signs. For the series of signs is divided among the natures of the four elements: water, fire, air, or earth. The East alone seems to see the nature of the individual born, but it is composed of many things in common. And when Aries <note>original: ♈︎</note> (fire) is rising, Libra <note>original: ♎︎</note> (air) is opposite, while Capricorn <note>original: ♑︎</note> (earth) and Cancer <note>original: ♋︎</note> (water) occupy the four cardines <term>cardines: the four pivotal points of the sky—the Ascendant, Descendant, Midheaven, and Imum Coeli</term>; these together create the complexions <term>complexions: the balance of bodily humors and temperament</term> of nature.

<margin>Also</margin> There is a distinction of colors through the quadrants of the circle. For between the East and the cardine of the earth <note>the bottom of the sky</note>, the color is red; from there to the seventh house, black; then to the tenth, green; the fourth is white. ¶ Furthermore, there is a certain variety of colors among the houses themselves. For the East itself is of a whitish color which they call gray. The second house is green on the outside; the third and eleventh are bluish-gray; the cardines are red; the fifth and ninth are white; the sixth and eighth are black; the seventh is dark.

<margin>Also</margin> There is a dimension of quantities through the quadrants of the circle. For from the East to the opposite point, the measure of brevity is taken; from the seventh house to the East, the measure of length. Furthermore, the two quadrants ascending from the bottom through the East to the top are increasing, while the remaining two are the opposite.

<image-desc>Decorative initial N with leafy flourishes and a central stalk.</image-desc>
**Now it seems necessary to explain** how the partitions of many things follow the order of nature through the number four. There are four modes: the regions of the winds, the seasons of the year, the signs and all parts of the circle, the complexions, the ages of man, and the quadrants of the day and night.
